let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}

  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

async function ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify=true}={}){
  uepDiagRecord('session.ensure.start',{verify,online:navigator.onLine});
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
    const missing={authenticated:false,reason:'School Read API 세션 기능 없음'};
    uepDiagRecord('session.bridge.missing',missing);return missing;
  }
  let status=null;
  try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify});uepDiagRecord('session.status.before',status);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.before.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  if(status?.authenticated)return status;
  const saved=state?.auth?.user||state?.auth?.rememberedUser||state?.settings?.userProfile||{};
  const name=String(saved?.name||state?.settings?.loginUserName||state?.settings?.teacherName||'').trim();
  const email=String(saved?.email||'').trim();
  uepDiagRecord('session.identity.available',{hasName:Boolean(name),hasEmail:Boolean(email),source:state?.auth?.user?'auth.user':state?.auth?.rememberedUser?'auth.rememberedUser':'settings.userProfile'});
  if(!name||!email||!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin){
    const missing={authenticated:false,reason:!name||!email?'저장된 담임 이름·이메일 없음':'School Read 로그인 기능 없음'};
    uepDiagRecord('session.login.skipped',missing);return status||missing;
  }
  let login=null;
  try{login=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadLogin({name,email});uepDiagRecord('session.login.result',login);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.login.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 예외'};}
  if(!login?.ok)return {authenticated:false,reason:login?.message||login?.reason||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 실패'};
  try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('session.status.after',status);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.after.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'로그인 후 세션 검증 예외'};}
  return status||{authenticated:false};
}

async function refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force=true, rerender=true}={}) {
  if(readonlyRefreshInFlight)return readonlyRefreshInFlight;

  try {
    if(force){
      const sync=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
      if(!sync?.ok) throw new Error(sync?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패');
    }
    const cached=await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
    if(!cached?.ok) throw new Error(cached?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.');
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError='';
    if(typeof studentBadgeCache!=='undefined'&&studentBadgeCache?.clear) studentBadgeCache.clear();
    updateDormOutingCount();
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if(rerender && !setupWizardActive){
      const scrollY=window.scrollY;
      render(state.activePage||'dashboard');
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.scrollTo(0,scrollY));
    }
    return true;
  } catch(error) {
    googleConnectionError=error?.message||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패';
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return false;
  }
  })();
  try{return await readonlyRefreshInFlight;}finally{readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;}
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function previewReadonlySync() {

  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){

  }

  catch(error){
    uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});
    result={ok:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 자료 조회 예외'};

    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const p = result.preview;
  const n = result.nightPreview || {};

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

      changed=true;
    }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }
  }
  if(!failed){ state.settings.programSheetMigrationV07662=true; changed=true; }
  if(changed) await save();
}

let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}

      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },

    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),

async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

  const credentialTask = window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus
    ? withStartupTimeout(ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}), 8000, null)
        .then((status) => {
          if(status) googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status.endpointReady!==false};
          if(!status?.authenticated && status?.reason) googleConnectionError=status.reason;
        })
        .catch((error) => {googleConnectionStatus={ok:false,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};googleConnectionError=error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 연결 실패';console.warn("[UEP] School Read API 최초 연결 실패", error);})
    : Promise.resolve();
  const cacheTask = window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache
    ? withStartupTimeout(window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache(), 8000, null)
        .then((cached) => {
          if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
          else if (cached) googleConnectionError = cached.reason || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
        })
        .catch((error) => {
          console.warn("[UEP] 읽기 캐시 불러오기 실패", error);
          googleConnectionError = error?.message || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
        })
    : Promise.resolve();
  await Promise.all([credentialTask, cacheTask]);
  // 화면은 로컬/메모리 캐시로 먼저 표시한 뒤, 실행당 한 번만 백그라운드 원격 동기화한다.

  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!readonlyCache||!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS;
    const jitter=needsFull?(2000+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000)):(500+Math.floor(Math.random()*2500));
    setTimeout(async()=>{
      const ok=needsFull
        ? await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true})
        : await refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
      if(ok){
        updateTopSyncStatus();
        if(currentRoleId()==='admin') migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce().catch(()=>{});
      }
    },jitter);
  }
  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok && readonlyCache) await migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce();
  updateDormOutingCount();
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  // 여기서는 백그라운드에서 최신 계정 유효성을 재검증한다.
  // 자동로그인 세션은 이미 화면을 열었으므로 네트워크 지연이 시작 화면을 막지 않는다.
  await Promise.race([
    initializeUserSessionGate(),

      }catch{}
      resolve(true);
    },1800))

  // 자동로그인 세션은 이미 화면을 열었으므로 네트워크 지연이 시작 화면을 막지 않는다.
  await Promise.race([
    initializeUserSessionGate(),

        /* 0.81.20: local remembered state never bypasses School Read session verification */
      }catch{}
      resolve(true);
    },1800))
  ]);
  if(authSessionReady && !resumedRememberedSession) navigate(state.activePage || "dashboard");
  if(false && !googleConnectionStatus?.ok && currentRoleId()==="admin"){
    // 인증정보가 없거나 복호화/유효성 검증에 실패한 경우에는 기존 setupCompleted 값과 무관하게
    // 데이터 연결 단계에서 멈춘다. 학생정보 화면을 먼저 열어 권한부여를 건너뛰지 않는다.
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(3),50);
  }else if(!state.settings.setupCompleted){
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(0),50);
  }else{
    setSetupWizardLock(false);
  }
}

function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return false;
  }

function formatNeisYmd(text) {
  const value = String(text || "");
  return /^\d{8}$/.test(value) ? `${Number(value.slice(4, 6))}/${Number(value.slice(6, 8))}` : value;
}

function periodTimeLabel(period) {
  const row = (state.settings.schoolHours || []).filter((item) => String(item?.[0] || "").includes("교시"))[Number(period) - 1];
  return row ? `${row[1]}~${row[2]}` : "";
}

function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
  if(!/^\d{8}$/.test(text))return null;
  const date=new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)),Number(text.slice(4,6))-1,Number(text.slice(6,8)),12,0,0);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?null:date;
}
function expectedSchoolPeriods(value){
  const date=value instanceof Date?value:neisDateToLocalDate(value);
  const weekdayNo=date?.getDay();
  return weekdayNo===3?5:([1,2,4,5].includes(weekdayNo)?7:0);
}

}
function uep08123FirstArray(...values){
  for(const value of values){ if(Array.isArray(value)&&value.length) return value; }
  return [];
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function previewReadonlySync() {
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync)return toast("학교 공용 데이터 연결 기능을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function updateConnectionControls() {
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const status = $(".connection-title .state");
  if (status && connected) {
    status.textContent = "구글시트 연결됨";
    status.classList.remove("warn", "error");
  }
  const sync = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (sync) sync.disabled = !googleConnectionStatus?.ok;
}

function updateDashboardTimetableControls() {
  const card = $(".timetable-card");
  if (!card) return;
  card.querySelectorAll(".timetable-popup-actions").forEach(node=>node.remove());
}

}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

function parseStudentLine(line) {
  const trimmed = String(line || "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return { number: "", name: "" };
  const parts = trimmed.includes("\t")
    ? trimmed.split(/\t+/)
    : trimmed.includes(",")
      ? trimmed.split(/,+/)
      : trimmed.split(/\s+/);
  return { number: (parts.shift() || "").trim(), name: parts.join(" ").trim() };
}

function openProgramSheetSettings() {
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GOOGLE SHEETS";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "프로그램 시트 연결 준비";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<form class="site-editor" id="programSheetForm"><label>구글시트 주소<input id="programSheetUrl" type="url" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.programSheetUrl || "")}" placeholder="https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/..."></label><label>프로그램 탭 이름<input id="programSheetName" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.programSheetName || "프로그램")}"></label><div class="sheet-schema"><b>권장 열 구조</b><span>운영일 · 프로그램명 · 시작시간 · 종료시간 · 장소 · 담당교사 · 학번 · 이름 · 안내</span></div><button class="btn primary">연결 정보 저장</button><p class="safe-note">여기서는 주소와 탭 이름만 저장합니다. 실제 읽기·쓰기는 권한 확인과 선생님 승인 후 연결합니다.</p></form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#programSheetForm").onsubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    state.settings.programSheetUrl = $("#programSheetUrl").value.trim();
    state.settings.programSheetName = $("#programSheetName").value.trim() || "프로그램";
    await save();
    toast("시트 연결 정보를 저장했습니다.");
    closeDrawer();
  };
}


function upsertNoticeCache(payload,id){
  readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};readonlyCache.notices=Array.isArray(readonlyCache.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[];
  const normalized={...payload,id:String(id||payload.id||''),status:payload.status||'게시',detail:payload.content,date:payload.postDate,receipts:readonlyCache.notices.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))?.receipts||[]};
  const index=readonlyCache.notices.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(normalized.id));
  if(index>=0)readonlyCache.notices[index]={...readonlyCache.notices[index],...normalized};else readonlyCache.notices.unshift(normalized);
  if(Array.isArray(state.workNotices)&&state.workNotices.length){state.workNotices=[];save().catch(()=>{});}
  return normalized;
}
function setNoticeBusy(form,busy,label='저장 중…'){
  const button=form?.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');if(!button)return;
  if(busy){button.dataset.originalText=button.textContent;button.disabled=true;button.textContent=label;form.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');}
  else{button.disabled=false;button.textContent=button.dataset.originalText||'저장';form.removeAttribute('aria-busy');}
}

/* UEP_PENDING_51_LIVE_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH_START */
(function installSelection51LiveRefresh(){
  if(typeof window==='undefined'||window.__UEPSelection51LiveRefreshInstalled)return;
  window.__UEPSelection51LiveRefreshInstalled=true;

  // 51 is generated outside UEP by Apps Script. The generic readonly cache may stay
  // valid for up to an hour, so selection screens must not trust its age.
  let inFlight=null;
  let lastStartedAt=0;
  const MIN_INTERVAL_MS=15000;

  const isSelectionWorkspace=()=>{
    try{
      if(String(state?.activePage||'')!=='records')return false;
      const mode=String(typeof curriculumWorkspaceMode!=='undefined'?curriculumWorkspaceMode:'');
      return mode==='students'||mode==='cross'||mode==='subjects';
    }catch{return false;}
  };

  async function refreshSelection51FromSheet(){
    if(inFlight)return inFlight;
    const now=Date.now();
    if(now-lastStartedAt<MIN_INTERVAL_MS)return false;
    lastStartedAt=now;
    inFlight=(async()=>{
      try{
        // Full readonly sync is intentional here: 51 lives in the data-processing
        // spreadsheet and is not part of the lightweight operational-range sync.
        // This guarantees readonlyCache.selectionErrorRows is rebuilt from current 51.
        const ok=await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:false});
        if(ok&&isSelectionWorkspace())render('records');
        return !!ok;
      }catch(error){
        console.warn('[UEP] 51 selection-error live refresh failed',error);
        return false;
      }
    })();
    try{return await inFlight;}finally{inFlight=null;}
  }

  const base=window.uepSelectionDataset||uepSelectionDataset;
  if(typeof base==='function'){
    const wrapped=function(){
      if(isSelectionWorkspace())queueMicrotask(()=>refreshSelection51FromSheet());
      return base.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    window.uepSelectionDataset=wrapped;
    try{uepSelectionDataset=wrapped}catch{}
  }

  // Exposed only for diagnostics/manual refresh from devtools; no sheet writes occur.
  window.__UEPRefreshSelection51=refreshSelection51FromSheet;
})();
/* UEP_PENDING_51_LIVE_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH_END */

function inputCenterExpandTextFile(file){
  const raw=String(file?.text||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
  const structured=inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file,raw);
  const classification=inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file,raw);
  if(structured){structured.forEach(d=>{const q=(d.sheetHeaders||[]).filter(h=>/^\s*\d+[.)]/.test(String(h||''))).length;const isReport=q>=3||/보고서|제출내역_텍스트/.test(String(d.sheetName||''));d.detectedType=isReport?'보고서·탐구보고서':classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=isReport?'높음':classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=isReport?`Excel 문항형 보고서 · ${q}개 문항 직접 인식`:classification.reason;});return structured;}
  if(!raw.trim()){const d=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,'');d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;return [d];}
  const lines=raw.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());
  const studentLines=lines.filter(line=>/(?:^|[^0-9])[123][0-9]{3}(?![0-9])/.test(line));
  const drafts=(studentLines.length>=2 && studentLines.length>=Math.min(3,Math.ceil(lines.length*.25)))?studentLines.map(line=>inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},line)):[inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,raw)];
  drafts.forEach(d=>{d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;});return drafts;
}

  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = name;

  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
}

function openConnectionDetail(key) {
  const item = state.settings.dataConnections?.[key];
  if (!item) return;
  const columns = key === "studentMaster"
    ? "학급 · 번호 · 성명 · 출신학교 · 입학자료 · 학생/보호자 연락처 · 생년월일 · 비고"
    : "학급 · 번호 · 성명 · 학사 방 · 퇴사 · 입사 · 학기별 변동";
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "READ ONLY SOURCE";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = item.label;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="connection-detail"><div><b>원본 파일</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.fileTitle)}</span></div><div><b>대상 탭</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.sheetName)}</span></div><div><b>머리글 행</b><span>${item.headerRow}행</span></div><div><b>결합 기준</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.joinKey)}</span></div><div><b>확인된 열</b><span>${columns}</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>현재 상태</b><span>구조 확인 완료 · OAuth 인증 전 · 실제 행 미저장</span></div><p class="drawer-note">동기화 시 학급+번호가 같더라도 성명이 다르면 자동 결합하지 않고 오류 목록으로 분리합니다.</p>`;

function dormView() {
  const currentStudents=readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[],outings=readonlyCache?.dormOutings||[];
  const termTabs=['g1s1','g1s2','g2s1','g2s2','g3'].map(mode=>{const term=dormTermDefinition(mode);return `<button data-dorm-mode="${mode}" class="${dormMode===mode?'active':''}">${term.label}</button>`;}).join('');
  const tabs=`<div class="attendance-tabs dorm-tabs dorm-growth-tabs">${termTabs}<button data-dorm-mode="outing" class="${dormMode==='outing'?'active':''}">학사운영규칙</button></div>`;
  if(dormMode==='outing'){
    if(false)setTimeout(()=>refreshDormRulesFromReadonly({rerender:true,silent:true}),0);
    return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${dormRuleManagementMarkup(currentStudents,outings)}</div>`;
  }
  const term=dormTermDefinition(dormMode)||dormTermDefinition('g1s2'),roster=dormTermRoster(dormMode);
  const cards=roster.map(row=>dormGrowthCard(row,term)).join('');
  return `<div class="module-page dorm-growth-page dorm-growth-page-compact">${tabs}${cards?`<div class="dorm-growth-grid">${cards}</div>`:`<div class="empty-state"><b>${term.label} 학사생 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>20_학사생마스터에 해당 시점의 학사생 기록이 쌓이면 이 위치에 자동 표시됩니다.</span></div>`}</div>`;
}

function attendanceStudentKey(row) {
  // 출결 원본은 학생ID가 비어 있거나 서로 다른 형식일 수 있으므로 학번을 최우선 기준으로 사용합니다.
  return String(row?.studentNo || row?.studentId || `${row?.name || ""}|${row?.className || ""}`);
}
function nightRecordIsAttendance(row) {
  const text = `${row?.result || ""} ${row?.detail || ""} ${row?.type || ""}`;
  if (/결석|미입실|미제출|불참/.test(text)) return false;
  return /출석|정상|명단QR|QR|입실/.test(text) || Boolean(row?.time || row?.period);
}
function openAttendanceHistory(kind, studentKey) {
  const source = kind === "late" ? (readonlyCache?.lateAttendance || []) : (readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || []);
  const rows = source.filter((row) => attendanceStudentKey(row) === String(studentKey)).sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)) || String(b.time || b.period || "").localeCompare(String(a.time || a.period || "")));
  const first = rows[0] || {};
  const student = (readonlyCache?.students || []).find((item) => item.id === first.studentId || String(item.studentNo) === String(first.studentNo));
  const titleName = student?.name || first.name || "학생";
  const studentNo = student?.studentNo || first.studentNo || "-";
  const className = student?.className || first.className || "-";
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = kind === "late" ? "LATE HISTORY" : "NIGHT STUDY HISTORY";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = `${studentNo} ${titleName}`;
  if (kind === "late") {
    const totalMinutes = rows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+Number(row.minutes||0),0);
    $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary"><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(className)}반</b></span><span><small>누적 지각</small><b>${rows.length}회</b></span><span><small>누적 시간</small><b>${totalMinutes}분</b></span><span><small>최근 지각</small><b>${escapeHtml(rows[0]?.date || "-")}</b></span></div><div class="data-card history-table"><div class="data-head late-history-grid"><span>일자</span><span>시각</span><s

function connectionHealthRows(){
  const required=[
    ["02_학생마스터",(readonlyCache?.students||[]).length>0],
    ["04_학생연락식별정보",(readonlyCache?.students||[]).some(s=>s.phone&&s.phone!=="-")],
    ["05_대학적성데이터",!String(googleConnectionError||"").includes("05_대학적성데이터")&&!String(googleConnectionError||"").includes("05_진로희망이력")],
    ["06_선택과목이력",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.subjectSelections)],
    ["20_학사생마스터",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormStudents)],
    ["41_학교캘린더",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.calendarEvents)],
    ["42_급식지도계획",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties)],
    ["43_야자감독계획",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors)]
  ];
  return required.map(([name,ok])=>({name,ok:Boolean(ok)}));
}
function issueStatusLabel(value){return ({new:"접수",checking:"확인 중",fixed:"수정 완료",hold:"보류"})[value]||"접수";}

const PRIVACY_MODE_STORE='uep-privacy-demo-mode-v1';
function privacyModeEnabled(){try{return localStorage.getItem(PRIVACY_MODE_STORE)==='1';}catch{return false;}}
function applyPrivacyMode(){
  const enabled=privacyModeEnabled();
  document.body.classList.toggle('privacy-demo-mode',enabled);
  const button=document.getElementById('privacyModeButton');
  if(button){
    button.classList.toggle('active',enabled);
    const b=button.querySelector('b'); if(b)b.textContent=enabled?'개인정보 보호 중':'개인정보 가리기';
    button.title=enabled?'개인정보 보호 모드 해제':'학생·연수 시연용 개인정보 가리기';
  }
  const signal=document.getElementById('studentSignalButton');
  if(signal&&enabled)signal.title='선택 학생 정보 보호 중';
  else if(signal)updateStudentSignalIndicator();
}
function togglePrivacyMode(){
  const next=!privacyModeEnabled();
  try{localStorage.setItem(PRIVACY_MODE_STORE,next?'1':'0');}catch{}
  applyPrivacyMode();
  toast(next?'개인정보 보호 모드를 켰습니다. 기능은 유지하고 주요 개인정보만 가립니다.':'개인정보 보호 모드를 해제했습니다.');
}

function dashboardTodayProgramVisible(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const title=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"").trim();
  const kind=String(program.kind||"");
  const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||program.targetGrade||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  // 대시보드 표시를 명시적으로 끈 프로그램은 어떤 출처에서도 다시 살리지 않는다.
  if(program.showTodayProgram===false||program.showOnDashboard===false||program.dashboardVisible===false||program.dashboardShow===false)return false;
  // 개학식·방학식 등 학교 전체 의식/행사는 '프로그램' 카드가 아니다.
  if(/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title))return false;
  if(kind==="calendar-first-grade")return calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(program)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(program);
  if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))return false;
  // 공통활동 중 대상 텍스트가 전교생/전체학생/전학년이면 제외한다.
  if(kind==="common"&&/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체|1학년전체/.test(target))return false;
  // 공통활동의 참여명단이 현재 재학생 대부분을 덮는 경우도 전교생 자동참여로 본다.
  if(kind==="common"&&Array.isArray(program.students)&&program.students.length){
    const active=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(studentIsCurrentlyActive);
    if(active.length>=20&&program.students.length>=Math.ceil(active.length*0.9))return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function deadlineBadge(item){
  if(item.days<0)return `D+${Math.abs(item.days)}`; if(item.days===0)return "오늘"; if(item.days===1)return "내일"; return `D-${item.days}`;
}
function automaticDeadlineMarkup(limit=20){
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true}).filter(item=>item.days>=-7&&item.days<=30).slice(0,limit);
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="work-empty"><b>현재 자동 마감공지가 없습니다.</b><small>프로그램 운영일·보고서·기관보고·학교업무 마감을 자동 계산해 표시합니다.</small></div>';
  return rows.map(item=>`<button class="work-auto-deadline ${item.days<0?'overdue':item.days<=1?'urgent':''}" data-auto-deadline-source="${escapeHtml(item.source)}" data-auto-deadline-id="${escapeHtml(String(item.id||''))}"><i>${escapeHtml(item.category)}</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.due)} · ${escapeHtml(item.meta||'')}</small></span><em>${deadlineBadge(item)}</em></button>`).join('');
}
function automaticDeadlineAdminReviewMarkup(limit=40){
  if(!currentUserCanManageNotices())return automaticDeadlineMarkup(limit);
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true,includeDismissed:true,includeUnapproved:true}).filter(item=>item.days>=-7&&item.days<=30).slice(0,limit);
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="work-empty"><b>검토할 자동공지 후보가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  const label=status=>status==='approved'?'승인':status==='handled'?'확인':status==='blocked'?'불가(해제)':'검토대기';
  return rows.map(item=>{const status=autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(item.source,item.id);return `<button class="work-auto-deadline admin-review ${status}" data-auto-deadline-source="${escapeHtml(item.source)}" data-auto-deadline-id="${escapeHtml(String(item.id||''))}"><i>${escapeHtml(item.category)}</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.due)} · ${escapeHtml(item.meta||'')} · ${label(status)}</small></span><em>${deadlineBadge(item)}</em></button>`;}).join('');
}

  return `<article class="card task-card operations-card operations-card-v07668 widget-configurable" ${widgetAttrs('operations')}><div class="card-head"><div><span class="card-icon ci-blue">✓</span><strong>학교업무공지·마감</strong></div><div class="operation-head-tools"><span><b>${notices.length}</b> 직접공지</span><span><b>${deadlines.length}</b> 자동마감</span><button class="text-button notice-add-button" data-notice-add>＋ 공지</button></div></div><div class="work-four-grid"><section><header><b>게시 중 직접공지</b><button data-page="work">전체</button></header>${noticeBody}</section><section><header><b>자동 마감공지</b><button data-page="work">전체</button></header>${deadlineBody}</section><section><header><b>상담 필요</b><button data-student-counsel-route>전체</button></header>${dashboardCounselMarkup()}</section><section><header><b>내 체크리스트</b><button data-page="work">전체</button></header>${checklistBody}</section></div></article>`;
}

function dashboardTodayProgramVisible(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const title=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"").trim();
  const kind=String(program.kind||"");
  const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||program.targetGrade||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  // 대시보드 표시를 명시적으로 끈 프로그램은 어떤 출처에서도 다시 살리지 않는다.
  if(program.showTodayProgram===false||program.showOnDashboard===false||program.dashboardVisible===false||program.dashboardShow===false)return false;
  // 개학식·방학식 등 학교 전체 의식/행사는 '프로그램' 카드가 아니다.
  if(/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title))return false;
  if(kind==="calendar-first-grade")return calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(program)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(program);
  if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))return false;
  // 공통활동 중 대상 텍스트가 전교생/전체학생/전학년이면 제외한다.
  if(kind==="common"&&/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체|1학년전체/.test(target))return false;
  // 공통활동의 참여명단이 현재 재학생 대부분을 덮는 경우도 전교생 자동참여로 본다.
  if(kind==="common"&&Array.isArray(program.students)&&program.students.length){
    const active=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(studentIsCurrentlyActive);
    if(active.length>=20&&program.students.length>=Math.ceil(active.length*0.9))return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function dashboardReportGroupState(group){
  const total=(group?.rows||[]).length;
  const submitted=(group?.rows||[]).filter(row=>row.submitted===true).length;
  return {total,submitted,missing:Math.max(0,total-submitted),state:total>0&&submitted===total?"complete":submitted>0?"progress":"missing"};
}
function dashboardProgramIsSelectedActivity(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const kind=String(program.kind||program.activityKind||program.programKind||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const label=[program.activityType,program.programType,program.category,program.group,program.type].map(value=>String(value||'')).join(' ');
  if(kind==='common'||kind==='after'||/공통활동|공통\s*프로그램|방과후/.test(label))return false;
  return kind==='selected'||kind==='general'||/선택활동|선택\s*프로그램/.test(label);
}
function dashboardReportStatusRows(){
  const rows=[];
  const programs=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(program=>dashboardProgramIsSelectedActivity(program));
  const reports=readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[];
  programs.forEach(program=>{
    const programTitle=String(program.recordTitle||program.actualTitle||program.title||'프로그램명 확인 필요').trim();
    (program.students||[]).forEach(student=>{
      // UEP 0.82.05: explicit report setting/form/data connection, or an actually submitted report,
      // is the only evidence that this student-program pair belongs to the report target population.
      if(!programRequiresStudentReport(program,student,reports))return;
      const matchedReport=resolveStudentProgramReport(program,student,reports);
      const submitted=Boolean(matchedReport&&matchedReport.submitted!==false);
      rows.push({
        studentId:student.studentId||student.id||'',
        studentNo:student.studentNo||student.number||'',
        name:student.name||student.studentName||'',
        classNo:student.classNo||student.className||'',
        programId:program.programId||program.id||'',
        programTitle,
        activityKind:'선택활동',
        submitted,
        reportId:matchedReport?.id||'',
        reportRef:matchedReport||null,
        status:submitted?'제출 완료':'미제출'
      });
    });
  });
  if(rows.length)return rows;
  // Fallback: if program roster metadata is temporarily unavailable, show submitted normalized reports only.
  return reports.filter(report=>dashboardProgramIsSelectedActivity(report)).map(report=>({
    ...report,
    programTitle:report.programTitle||report.programName||report.reportGroup||'프로그램명 확인 필요',
    activityKind:'선택활동',
    submitted:report.submitted!==false&&Boolean(report.id||report.reportId||report.submittedAt||report.content)
  }));
}

    ['progress','제출중',`${progress.length}`,''],
    ['report','미제출',`${missing.length}`,'']
  ];
  return `<div class="uep-student-status-compact">${items.map(([kind,label,value,cls])=>`<button class="${cls}" data-dashboard-student-status="${kind}" title="${label} 상세보기"><span>${label}</span><b>${value}</b></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

===== FILE: app\resources\app\electron\google-data.cjs | PATTERN: 20_학사생마스터 =====
of Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
    return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  if (typeof value === "number") {
    const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
    return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }
  const text = String(value).trim();
  if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
  const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
}

function classNumberSafe(value) { return String(value || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); }

function parseGoogleSheetData(sheetMatrices) {
  const rowsFrom = (name) => matrixToObjectRows(sheetMatrices[name] || []);
  const masters = rowsFrom("02_학생마스터").filter(
    (row) => String(row["활성여부"] || "Y").trim() !== "N",
  );
  const contactSourceRows = rowsFrom("04_학생연락식별정보");
  const contacts = new Map(
    contactSourceRows
      .filter((row) => String(row["현재사용여부"] || "Y").trim() !== "N")
      .map((row) => [String(row["학생ID"] || "").trim(), row]),
  );
  const dormRows = rowsFrom("20_학사생마스터");
  const currentDorm = new Map(
    dormRows
      .filter(
        (row) =>
          String(row["현재여부"] || "").trim() === "Y" &&
          String(row["학사상태"] || "").trim() === "재사",
      )
      .map((row) => [String(row["학생ID"] || "").trim(), row]),
  );

    const aptitude=String(row["적성"] || row["적성유형"] || row["강점"] || "").trim();
    return [id,{career,major,track,interest,aptitude}];
  }));
  const careers = new Map([...careerProfiles].map(([id,p]) => [id, [p.career,p.major].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")]));

      householdQualification: String(row["가구자격"] || "").trim(),
      incomePercent: String(row["중위소득(%)"] || "").trim(),
      baseDate: localDate(row["기준일"]),

async function migrateStableUserFile(fileName) {
  const target=path.join(stableUserDataRoot(),fileName), legacy=path.join(legacyUserDataRoot(),fileName);
  try { await fs.access(target); return; } catch {}
  try { await fs.mkdir(stableUserDataRoot(),{recursive:true}); await fs.copyFile(legacy,target); } catch {}
}

  // Local State에는 Windows safeStorage가 사용하는 Chromium OS encryption key 정보가 들어 있습니다.
  // 기존 버전의 userData에서 최초 1회 같이 옮겨야 기존 암호문을 복호화할 가능성이 유지됩니다.
  for (const fileName of ["Local State","school-board-state.json","quick-launch-settings.json","google-service-account.bin","google-service-account.recovery.json","google-user-oauth.bin","google-user-oauth.recovery.json","neis-api-key.bin","neis-dashboard-cache.json","comcigan-timetable-cache.json"]) await migrateStableUserFile(fileName);
}

async function appendShutdownLog(message) {
  try {
    const file = path.join(stableUserDataRoot(), "shutdown-diagnostics.log");
    await fs.mkdir(stableUserDataRoot(), { recursive: true });
    await fs.appendFile(file, `${new Date().toISOString()} ${message}\n`, "utf8");
  } catch {}
}
function destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason="shutdown") {
  try { appendShutdownLog(`destroy-aux:${reason}:comcigan=${Boolean(comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed())}`); } catch {}
  try {
    if (comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed()) comciganWindow.destroy();
  } catch {}
  comciganWindow = null;
}

===== FILE: app\resources\app\electron\main.cjs | PATTERN: schoolRead =====
JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.
  try {
    const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
    if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, legacy); return legacy; }
  } catch {}
  // Google 서비스 계정은 safeStorage가 깨져도 동일 PC/사용자용 AES-GCM 복구본으로 자동 복원합니다.
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
    try {
      const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
      if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
        try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
        return recovered;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
  try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
  const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
  error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
  throw error;
}

const UEP_SCHOOL_READ_API_FALLBACK='https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbyLEEam4L2sX653pii69TC3_84wTyfAed_UL8HRY6PBG467im1LmZJ0CIpSKSOj4r6-0w/exec';
let schoolReadPolicyCache={url:'',expiresAt:0};
function schoolReadError(message,code='UEP_SCHOOL_READ_ERROR'){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadSchoolReadApiUrl(){
  if(schoolReadPolicyCache.url&&schoolReadPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now())return schoolReadPolicyCache.url;
  let response;
  try{response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:'no-store'});}catch(error){throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE');}
  if(!response.ok)throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 설정 조회 실패 ('+response.status+')','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE');
  const policy=await response.json();
  const url=String(policy?.schoolReadApiUrl||UEP_SCHOOL_READ_API_FALLBACK||'').trim();
  if(!/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/s\/[^/]+\/exec(?:\?.*)?$/i.test(url))throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 배포 URL이 아직 등록되지 않았습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_NOT_CONFIGURED');
  schoolReadPolicyCache={url,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return url;
}
async function getOrCreateSchoolReadDeviceId(){
  try{const saved=String(await fs.readFile(schoolReadDevicePath(),'utf8')).trim();if(saved)return saved;}catch{}
  const id='UEP-'+crypto.randomUUID();
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(schoolReadDevicePath()),{recursive:true});
  await fs.writeFile(schoolReadDevicePath(),id,'utf8');
  return id;
}

}

unction classifyCalendarStatus(text) {
  const value=String(text||"");
  if(/취소|폐지/.test(value)) return "취소";
  if(/확정/.test(value)) return "확정";
  if(/예약|대관|사용/.test(value)) return "예약";
  if(/예정|계획|공고|제출기간/.test(value)) return "예정";
  return "상태 미기재";
}
function parseOwner(text) {
  const m=String(text||"").match(/^\(([^)]+)\)/);
  const token=(m?.[1]||"").trim();
  if(!token) return ["",""];
  if(/부$|부\s/.test(token)) return [token.replace(/\s*확정$/,""),""];
  const parts=token.split(/\s+/);
  if(parts.length>1 && /부$/.test(parts[0])) return [parts[0],parts.slice(1).join(" ")];
  return ["",token.replace(/\s*확정$/," ").trim()];
}
async function sheetsRequest(token,url,options={}) {
  const response=await fetch(url,{...options,headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"content-type":"application/json",...(options.headers||{})}});
  if(!response.ok){let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""}catch{};throw new Error(`Google Sheets 쓰기 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`)}
  return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
}
async function replaceCalendarRows(token, rows=[]) {
  const base=`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID}/values`;
  await sheetsRequest(token,`${base}/${encodeURIComponent("'41_학교캘린더'!A4:W3000")}:clear`,{method:"POST",body:"{}"});
  if(rows.length) await sheetsRequest(token,`${base}/${encodeURIComponent(`'41_학교캘린더'!A4:W${rows.length+3}`)}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`,{method:"PUT",body:JSON.stringify({majorDimension:"ROWS",values:rows})});
}

async function currentCalendarRows(token) {

}
function calendarObjectToRow(row){
  const start=String(row["시작일"]||row["일자"]||"");
  return [row["일정ID"],row["학년도"]||start.slice(0,4),start,row["종료일"]||row["종료일자"]||start,row["시작시각"],row["종료시각"],row["종일"]||(!row["시작시각"]?"Y":"N"),row["일정명"]||row["프로그램명"],row["상세내용"]||row["비고"],row["일정구분"]||"업무",row["대상"],row["장소"],row["부서"],row["담당자"],row["상태"]||"확정",row["검증상태"]||"정상",row["출처"]||"UEP 직접입력",row["원본시작셀"],row["원본끝셀"],row["원본일정ID"],row["이관방식"]||"직접기록",row["수정일시"],row["비고"]];
}


function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

async function schoolReadSessionStatus({verify=true}={}){
  let endpointReady=true,endpointError='';
  try{await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();}catch(error){endpointReady=false;endpointError=String(error?.message||error);}
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)return {ok:endpointReady,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:endpointError,user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  if(!verify)return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady,user:session.user||null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  try{const result=await schoolReadPost('verify-token',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId});if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'UEP 세션 확인 실패',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_TOKEN_INVALID');session.user=result.user||session.user||{};await saveSchoolReadSession(session);return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady:true,user:session.user,expiresAt:result.expiresAt||'',mode:'school_read_api'};}catch(error){await clearSchoolReadSession();return {ok:false,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:String(error?.message||error),code:error?.code||'',user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};}
}
async function schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges){
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)throw schoolReadError('UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_LOGIN_REQUIRED');
  const result=await schoolReadPost('batch-read',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId,spreadsheetId,ranges});
  if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BATCH_FAILED');
  return result.valueRanges||[];
}

const UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-policy.json";

function googleOAuthError(message,code){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
  if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
  let response;
  try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
  catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 연결 설정 조회 실패 (${response.status})`,"UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE");
  const policy=await response.json();
  const clientId=String(policy?.googleOAuthClientId||"").trim();
  if(!/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/i.test(clientId)) throw googleOAuthError("관리자가 UEP Google OAuth 클라이언트를 아직 설정하지 않았습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return clientId;
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.statu

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value])

async function schoolReadSessionStatus({verify=true}={}){
  let endpointReady=true,endpointError='';
  try{await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();}catch(error){endpointReady=false;endpointError=String(error?.message||error);}
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)return {ok:endpointReady,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:endpointError,user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  if(!verify)return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady,user:session.user||null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  try{const result=await schoolReadPost('verify-token',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId});if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'UEP 세션 확인 실패',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_TOKEN_INVALID');session.user=result.user||session.user||{};await saveSchoolReadSession(session);return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady:true,user:session.user,expiresAt:result.expiresAt||'',mode:'school_read_api'};}catch(error){await clearSchoolReadSession();return {ok:false,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:String(error?.message||error),code:error?.code||'',user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};}
}
async function schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges){
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)throw schoolReadError('UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_LOGIN_REQUIRED');
  const result=await schoolReadPost('batch-read',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId,spreadsheetId,ranges});
  if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BATCH_FAILED');
  return result.valueRanges||[];
}

function base64url(value) { return Buffer.from(value).toString("base64url"); }

const UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-policy.json";
let googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:"",expiresAt:0};

function googleOAuthError(message,code){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
  if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
  let response;
  try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
  catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 연결 설정 조회 실패 (${response.status})`,"UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE");
  const policy=await response.json();
  const clientId=String(policy?.googleOAuthClientId||"").trim();
  if(!/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/i.test(clientId)) throw googleOAuthError("관리자가 UEP Google OAuth 클라이언트를 아직 설정하지 않았습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return clientId;
}

function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
  const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);
}
function encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
  const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
  const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);
  return {version:1,algorithm:'aes-256-gcm',salt:salt.toStr

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }

function writeNormalized_(rows) {
  writeSnapshot_(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET, rows, 25, {
    dateColumns: [2, 11, 14, 25],
    timeColumns: [15, 16, 17],
  });
}

function writeDaily_(rows) {
  writeSnapshot_(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET, rows, 30, {
    dateColumns: [3, 20, 21, 23, 30],
    timeColumns: [12, 13, 14],
  });
}

function writeSnapshot_(sheetName, rows, width, formats) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  const oldRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  const clearRows = Math.max(oldRows, rows.length);
  if (clearRows > 0) sheet.getRange(3, 1, clearRows, width).clearContent();

  if (!rows.length) return;
  sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);

  (formats.dateColumns || []).forEach((col) => {
    sheet.getRange(3, col, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  });
  (formats.timeColumns || []).forEach((col) => {
    sheet.getRange(3, col, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
  });
}

function writeErrors_(rows) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET);
  const width = 10;
  const oldRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  const clearRows = Math.max(oldRows, rows.length);
  if (clearRows > 0) sheet.getRange(3, 1, clearRows, width).clearContent();
  if (rows.length) {
    sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);
    sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  }
}

function markDailyAsSent_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET);
  const count = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  if (!count) return;
  const now = new Date();
  sheet.getRange(3, 22, count, 1).setValue('전송완료');
  sheet.getRange(3, 23, count, 1).setValue(now).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
}

function readNormalizedRows_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 3) return [];
  return sheet.getRange(3, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 2, 25).getValues()
    .filter((r) => r[0]);
}

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 3) return [];
  return sheet.getRange(3, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 2, 23).getValues()
    .filter

function loadDormOutingContext_() {
  validateRequiredSheets_();
  const config = getConfig_();

  const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID'));
  const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SHEET'));
  if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error('원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다.');

  const sourceBundle = readDormOutingSource_(sourceSheet, config);

  const targetSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(requiredConfig_(config, 'TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID'));
  const studentSheet = targetSs.getSheetByName(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SHEET || '02_학생마스터');
  const dormSheet = targetSs.getSheetByName(config.DORM_MASTER_SHEET || '20_학사생마스터');
  if (!studentSheet) throw new Error('02_학생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다.');
  if (!dormSheet) throw new Error('20_학사생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다.');

  return {
    config,
    sourceRows: sourceBundle.table,
    sourceMode: sourceBundle.mode,
    students: readStudentMaster_(studentSheet),
    dormStudents: readDormMaster_(dormSheet),
  };
}

function configureSourceFormSafety_() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID'));
  const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SHEET'));
  if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error('원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다.');

  const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
  if (!formUrl) {
    throw new Error('학사외출 응답 탭에 연결된 구글폼 URL을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  }

  const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
  form.setAllowResponseEdits(false);
  setConfigValue_('SOURCE_FORM_ID', form.getId());
  setConfigValue_('SOURCE_READ_MODE', 'FORM_DIRECT');
  setConfigValue_('ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK', 'FALSE');
  setConfigValue_('FORM_RESPONSE_EDITING', 'DISABLED');
}

function readDormOutingSource_(sourceSheet, config) {
  const mode = text_(config.SOURCE_READ_MODE || 'FORM_DIRECT').toUpperCase();
  if (mode === 'FORM_DIRECT') {
    const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
    if (formUrl) {
      const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
      const table = readFormResponsesAsTable_(form);
      syncDormOutingCollectionSheet_(table);
      return {
        mode: 'FORM_DIRECT',
        table,
      };
    }

    const allowFallback = text_(config.ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
    if (!allowFallback) {
      throw new Error('연결된 구글폼을 찾지 못해 원본 응답을 직접 읽을 수 없습니다.');
    }
  }

  return {
    mode: 'SHEET_FALLBACK',
    table: readTableFromRow1_(sourceSheet),
  };
}

  const responses = form.getResponses();
  const titles = [];

  responses.forEach((response) => {
    response.getItemResponses().forEach((itemResponse

        table,
      };

function readFormResponsesAsTable_(form) {
  const responses = form.getResponses();
  const titles = [];

function writeNormalized_(rows) {

    dateColumns: [2, 11, 14, 25],
    timeColumns: [15, 16, 17],

function writeDaily_(rows) {
  writeSnapshot_(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET, rows, 30, {
    dateColumns: [3, 20, 21, 23, 30],
    timeColumns: [12, 13, 14],
  });
}

function writeSnapshot_(sheetName, rows, width, formats) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  const oldRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  const clearRows = Math.max(oldRows, rows.length);
  if (clearRows > 0) sheet.getRange(3, 1, clearRows, width).clearContent();

  if (!rows.length) return;
  sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);

  (formats.dateColumns || []).forEach((col) => {
    sheet.getRange(3, col, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  });
  (formats.timeColumns || []).forEach((col) => {
    sheet.getRange(3, col, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
  });
}

function writeErrors_(rows) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET);
  const width = 10;
  const oldRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  const clearRows = Math.max(oldRows, rows.length);
  if (clearRows > 0) sheet.getRange(3, 1, clearRows, width).clearContent();
  if (rows.length) {
    sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);
    sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  }
}

function readNormalizedRows_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 3) return [];
  return sheet.getRange(3, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 2, 25).getValues()
    .filter((r) => r[0]);
}

function readDailyRows_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 3) return [];
  return sheet.getRange(3, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 2, 23).getValues()
    .filter((r) => r[0]);
}

function readTableFromRow1_(sheet) {
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 1 || sheet.getLastColumn() < 1) {
    return { headers: [], data: [] };
  }
  const values = sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getLastRow(), sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues();
  return {
    headers: values[0].map(text_),
    data: values.slice(1),
  };
}

function loadDormOutingContext_() {
  validateRequiredSheets_();
  const config = getConfig_();

  const sourceId = text_(config.SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.COLLECTION_SPREADSHEET_ID;
  const sourceSheetName = text_(config.SOURCE_FORM_SHEET) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.COLLECTION_SHEET;

  const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(sourceId);
  const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(sourceSheetName);
  if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error(`원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다: ${sourceSheetName}`);

  const sourceBundle = readDormOutingSource_(sourceSheet, config);

  // 기본정보 연결시트는 학생ID/재사생 판정을 위한 읽기 전용 참조입니다.
  // 과거 설정키 TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID가 남아 있어도 절대 학사외출 데이터를 쓰지 않습니다.
  const masterSpreadsheetId =
    text_(config.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT;

  const masterSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(masterSpreadsheetId);
  const studentSheetName = text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SHEET) || '02_학생마스터';
  const dormSheetName = text_(config.DORM_MASTER_SHEET) || '20_학사생마스터';
  const studentSheet = masterSs.getSheetByName(studentSheetName);
  const dormSheet = masterSs.getSheetByName(dormSheetName);

  if (!studentSheet) throw new Error(`학생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다: ${studentSheetName}`);
  if (!dormSheet) throw new Error(`학사생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다: ${dormSheetName}`);

  return {
    config,
    sourceRows: sourceBundle.table,
    sourceMode: sourceBundle.mode,
    students: readStudentMaster_(studentSheet),
    dormStudents: readDormMaster_(dormSheet),
    masterSpreadsheetId,
  };
}
function configureSourceFormSafety_() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID'));
  const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SHEET'));
  if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error('원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다.');

  const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
  if (!formUrl) {
    throw new Error('학사외출 응답 탭에 연결된 구글폼 URL을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  }

  const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
  form.setAllowResponseEdits(false);
  setConfigValue_('SOURCE_FORM_ID', form.getId());
  setConfigValue_('SOURCE_READ_MODE', 'FORM_DIRECT');
  setConfigValue_('ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK', 'FALSE');
  setConfigValue_('FORM_RESPONSE_EDITING', 'DISABLED');
}

    const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();

      const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
      const table = readFormResponsesAsTable_(form);

    }
  }

  return {
    mode: 'SHEET_FALLBACK',
    table: readTableFromRow1_(sourceSheet),
  };
}

function readFormResponsesAsTable_(form) {
  const responses = form.getResponses();
  const titles = [];

  responses.forEach((response) => {
    response.getItemResponses().forEach((itemResponse) => {
      const title = text_(itemResponse.getItem().getTitle());
      if (title && !titles.includes(title)) titles.push(title);
    });
  });

  const headers = ['타임스탬프', '__FORM_RESPONSE_ID__'].concat(titles);
  const data = responses.map((response) => {
    const answers = {};
    response.getItemResponses()
