-dd hh:mm');
  } else {
    sheet.appendRow([key, value, '스크립트 자동 생성 설정', true, new Date()]);
  }
}

function requiredConfig_(config, key) {
  const value = text_(config[key]);
  if (!value) throw new Error(`00_처리설정의 ${key} 값이 없습니다.`);
  return value;
}

function validateRequiredSheets_() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  [
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.CONFIG_SHEET,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.LOG_SHEET,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.RETRY_SHEET,
  ].forEach((name) => {
    if (!ss.getSheetByName(name)) throw new Error(`필수 탭 누락: ${name}`);
  });
}

function showUepDormOutingProcessorStatus() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  const normalized = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const daily = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const errors = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers()
    .filter((t) => t.getHandlerFunction() === UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER).length;

  const masterSpreadsheetId =
    text_(config.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT;

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    'UEP 학사외출 처리 상태',
    [
      `스크립트 버전: ${UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.VERSION}`,
      `설치상태: ${config.SCRIPT_STATUS || '미설치'}`,
      `자동 트리거: ${triggers}개`,
      `정규화 영구원장: ${Math.max(0, normalized)}건`,
      `일자별·규칙판정: ${Math.max(0, daily)}건`,
      `오류검토: ${Math.max(0, errors)}건`,

      `현재 폼 응답 수: ${config.LAST_SOURCE_FORM_SYNC_COUNT || '0'}`,
      `학생/학사생 참조: ${masterSpreadsheetId}`,
      `처리경로: FORM → 정규화 → 일자별 → UEP 직접조회`,
      `중복정책: 동일 논리신청 최신 제출 우선`,

      `최근 메일: ${config.LAST_DORM_MAIL_AT || '-'}`,
    ].join('\n'),
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

pDormOutingProcessorStatus() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  const normalized = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const daily = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const errors = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers()
    .filter((t) => t.getHandlerFunction() === UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER).length;

  const masterSpreadsheetId =
    text_(config.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||

    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT;

function rowToObject_(headers, row) {
  const obj = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (h) obj[h] = row[i];
  });
  return obj;
}

function firstValue_(obj, keys) {
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;
    const value = obj[key];
    if (value !== '' && value !== null && typeof value !== 'undefined') return value;
  }
  return '';
}

function firstDate_(obj, keys) {
  return toDateOnly_(firstValue_(obj, keys));
}

function firstTime_(obj, keys) {
  return formatTime_(firstValue_(obj, keys));
}

function parseIdentity_(value) {
  const match = text_(value).match(/^(\d+)반\s+(\d+)번\s+(.+)$/);
  if (!match) return null;
  return { classNo: match[1], studentNo: match[2], name: match[3].trim() };
}

function buildResponseId_(timestamp, identity, type, category, date, rowNumber) {
  const base = [
    formatDateTime_(timestamp),
    text_(identity),
    type,
    category,
    formatDate_(date),
    rowNumber,
  ].join('|');
  return `FRESP-${shortHash_(base)}-${rowNumber}`;
}

function shortHash_(value) {
  const bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(
    Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
    String(value),
    Utilities.Charset.UTF_8
  );
  return bytes.slice(0, 8)
    .map((b) => ((b + 256) % 256).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
}

function loadDormOutingContext_() {
  validateRequiredSheets_();
  const config = getConfig_();

  const sourceId = text_(config.SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.COLLECTION_SPREADSHEET_ID;
  const sourceSheetName = text_(config.SOURCE_FORM_SHEET) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.COLLECTION_SHEET;

  const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(sourceId);
  const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(sourceSheetName);
  if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error(`원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다: ${sourceSheetName}`);

  const sourceBundle = readDormOutingSource_(sourceSheet, config);

  // 기본정보 연결시트는 학생ID/재사생 판정을 위한 읽기 전용 참조입니다.
  // 과거 설정키 TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID가 남아 있어도 절대 학사외출 데이터를 쓰지 않습니다.
  const masterSpreadsheetId =
    text_(config.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT;

  const masterSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(masterSpreadsheetId);
  const studentSheetName = text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SHEET) || '02_학생마스터';
  const dormSheetName = text_(config.DORM_MASTER_SHEET) || '20_학사생마스터';
  const studentSheet = masterSs.getSheetByName(studentSheetName);
  const dormSheet = masterSs.getSheetByName(dormSheetName);

  if (!studentSheet) throw new Error(`학생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다: ${studentSheetName}`);
  if (!dormSheet) throw new Error(`학사생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다: ${dormSheetName}`);

  return {
    config,
    sourceRows: sourceBundle.table,
    sourceMode: sourceBundle.mode,
    students: readStudentMaster_(studentSheet),
    dormStudents: readDormMaster_(dormSheet),
    masterSpreadsheetId,
  };
}
function configureSourceFormSafety_() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID'));
  const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SHEET'));
  if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error('원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다.');

  const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
  if (!formUrl) {
    throw new Error('학사외출 응답 탭에 연결된 구글폼 URL을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  }

  const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
  form.setAllowResponseEdits(false);
  setConfigValue_('SOURCE_FORM_ID', form.getId());
  setConfigValue_('SOURCE_READ_MODE', 'FORM_DIRECT');
  setConfigValue_('ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK', 'FALSE');
  setConfigValue_('FORM_RESPONSE_EDITING', 'DISABLED');
}

function readDormOutingSource_(sourceSheet, config) {
  const mode = text_(config.SOURCE_READ_MODE || 'FORM_DIRECT').toUpperCase();
  if (mode === 'FORM_DIRECT') {
    const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
    if (formUrl) {
      const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
      const table = readFormResponsesAsTable_(form);
      syncDormOutingCollectionSheet_(table);
      return {
        mode: 'FORM_DIRECT',
        table,
      };
    }

    const allowFallback = text_(config.ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
    if (!allowFallback) {
      throw new Error('연결된 구글폼을 찾지 못해 원본 응답을 직접 읽을 수 없습니다.');
    }
  }

  return {
    mode: 'SHEET_FALLBACK',
    table: readTableFromRow1_(sourceSheet),
  };
}
